  <li><strong>Link:</strong> <a href="https://hv.varbi.com/se/what:job/jobID:989542/type:job/where:3/apply:1?token=eb670c3471dcdd0d717340c7c27579a2">View job posting</a></li>

  <li><strong>Type:</strong> Other</li>
  <li><strong>Deadline:</strong> 2026-12-02</li>
</ul>
</div></div>

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
  const countryUniversityMap = {"Denmark": ["Aalborg University", "Aarhus University", "Copenhagen Business School", "DTU", "IT University of Copenhagen", "Roskilde University", "University of Copenhagen", "University of Southern Denmark"], "Finland": ["Aalto University", "University of Eastern Finland", "University of Jyväskylä", "University of Lapland", "University of Oulu", "University of Turku", "University of Vaasa", "Åbo Akademi University"], "Norway": ["NMBU", "Nord University", "NTNU", "UiT The Arctic University of Norway", "University of Agder", "University of Bergen", "University of Inland Norway", "University of Oslo", "University of South-Eastern Norway"], "Sweden": ["Blekinge Institute of Technology", "Chalmers University of Technology", "Dalarna University", "Gothenburg University", "Halmstad University", "Karlstad University", "Karolinska Institute", "KTH", "Linköping University", "Linnaeus University", "Luleå University of Technology", "Lund University", "Malmö University", "Mid Sweden University", "Mälardalen University", "SLU", "Stockholm School of Economics", "Stockholm University", "Umeå University", "University West", "Uppsala University", "Örebro University"]};

  const countrySelect = document.getElementById('filterCountry');
  const universitySelect = document.getElementById('filterUniversity');
  const typeSelect = document.getElementById('filterType');
  const textInput = document.getElementById('jobFilter');
  const sortSelect = document.getElementById('sortJobs');
  const jobList = document.getElementById('jobList');
  const jobBlocks = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.job'));

  jobBlocks.forEach((job, index) => {
    job.dataset.originalIndex = index;
    const titleEl = job.querySelector('h3');
    if (titleEl) {  
      titleEl.dataset.originalText = titleEl.textContent;
    }
  });

  function escapeRegExp(string) {
    return string.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
  }

  function highlightText(job, query) {
    const titleEl = job.querySelector('h3');
    if (!titleEl) return;
    
    const original = titleEl.dataset.originalText || titleEl.textContent;
    titleEl.dataset.originalText = original;
    
    if (!query) {
        titleEl.textContent = original;
        return;
    }
    
    const safeQuery = escapeRegExp(query);
    const regex = new RegExp(`(${safeQuery})`, 'gi');
    
    titleEl.innerHTML = original.replace(regex, '<mark>$1</mark>');
  }

  function updateUniversityOptions() {
    const selectedCountry = countrySelect.value;
    const currentUniversity = universitySelect.value;

    let universities = [];

    if (selectedCountry) {
      universities = countryUniversityMap[selectedCountry] || [];
    } else {
      universities = Object.values(countryUniversityMap).flat();
    }

    const uniqueUniversities = [...new Set(universities)].sort((a, b) =>
      a.localeCompare(b)
    );

    universitySelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">All Universities</option>';

    uniqueUniversities.forEach((uni) => {
      const option = document.createElement('option');
      option.value = uni;
      option.textContent = uni;
      universitySelect.appendChild(option);
    });

    if (uniqueUniversities.includes(currentUniversity)) {
      universitySelect.value = currentUniversity;
    } else {
      universitySelect.value = '';
    }
  }

  function updateDisplay() {
    const selectedCountry = countrySelect.value.toLowerCase();
    const selectedUniversity = universitySelect.value.toLowerCase();
    const selectedType = typeSelect.value.toLowerCase();
    const query = textInput.value.trim().toLowerCase();
    const visibleJobs = [];
    const hiddenJobs = [];

    jobBlocks.forEach(job => {

      const country = (job.dataset.country || "").toLowerCase();
      const university = (job.dataset.university || "").toLowerCase();
      const jobType = (job.dataset.type || "").toLowerCase();
      const titleEl = job.querySelector('h3');
      const titleText = titleEl ? (titleEl.dataset.originalText || titleEl.textContent).toLowerCase() : "";

      const matchesCountry = !selectedCountry || country === selectedCountry;
      const matchesUniversity = !selectedUniversity || university === selectedUniversity;
      const matchesType = !selectedType || jobType === selectedType;
      const matchesQuery = !query || titleText.includes(query);

      if (matchesCountry && matchesUniversity && matchesType && matchesQuery) {
        job.style.display = '';
        highlightText(job, query);
        visibleJobs.push(job);
      } else {
        job.style.display = 'none';
        hiddenJobs.push(job);
      }
    });

    sortJobs(visibleJobs, hiddenJobs);
  }

  function compareByOriginalIndex(a, b) {
    return Number(a.dataset.originalIndex) - Number(b.dataset.originalIndex);
  }

  function compareByDeadline(a, b, direction) {
    const deadlineA = a.dataset.deadline || "";
    const deadlineB = b.dataset.deadline || "";

    if (!deadlineA && !deadlineB) return compareByOriginalIndex(a, b);
    if (!deadlineA) return 1;
    if (!deadlineB) return -1;

    const result = deadlineA.localeCompare(deadlineB);
    return direction === 'asc' ? result : -result;
  }

  function sortJobs(visibleJobs, hiddenJobs) {
    const orderedVisibleJobs = [...visibleJobs];
    const sortMode = sortSelect.value;

    if (sortMode === 'deadline-asc') {
      orderedVisibleJobs.sort((a, b) =>
        compareByDeadline(a, b, 'asc') || compareByOriginalIndex(a, b)
      );
    } else if (sortMode === 'deadline-desc') {
      orderedVisibleJobs.sort((a, b) =>
        compareByDeadline(a, b, 'desc') || compareByOriginalIndex(a, b)
      );
    } else {
      orderedVisibleJobs.sort(compareByOriginalIndex);
    }

    [...orderedVisibleJobs, ...hiddenJobs].forEach(job => jobList.appendChild(job));
  }

  countrySelect.addEventListener('change', () => {
    updateUniversityOptions();
    updateDisplay();
  });

  universitySelect.addEventListener('change', updateDisplay);
  typeSelect.addEventListener('change', updateDisplay);
  textInput.addEventListener('input', updateDisplay);
  sortSelect.addEventListener('change', updateDisplay);

  updateUniversityOptions();
  updateDisplay();
});
</script>
